# Glyph Performance Database — @wasglyph uploads + niche pop-offs

One database, two parts. **Part 1 — @wasglyph uploads:** every upload's numbers, logged the same way every time by the upload-radar (every 12h) — patterns about what actually moves this channel (retention bars, sub conversion, debate heat) that no single check can show. **Part 2 — Niche pop-offs:** every competitor blow-up (~3x+ baseline) the daily tripwire catches, with hook/mechanic breakdowns. Merged Oct 20, 2026 at Sal's request so ChatGPT gets both from this one file.

Seeded Oct 15, 2026 from morning briefings and upload-radar notes (figures approximate, labeled as briefing snapshots — not Studio-verified to the decimal). Fresh entries from the radar are Studio-verified.

## Part 1 — @wasglyph uploads

## Entry format (every upload gets all fields)

- **Upload date:**
- **Title:**
- **Check-ins:** dated lines — age, views, % stayed, avg % viewed, AVD, video-attributed net subs, comments
- **vs 82% bar:** stayed% relative to the @wasglyph breakout bar
- **Verdict:** breakout / near-miss / holding / dying
- **Format notes:** dilemma mechanic, hook type, debate vs retention play
- **Tags:**

## Part 1 — Emerging patterns

> **AUDIT NOTE (Oct 20, 2026, Mission 002):** Pattern labels below use the retired Oct 2026 standard ("confirmed" at 3+ instances). Under the current standard (methodology/research_rules.md §11–13), every pattern here is a **hypothesis** with a confidence level — see analysis/hypotheses.md for the scored register, counterexamples, and independent-video sample sizes. Original text preserved; nothing deleted.

(Update as entries accumulate. A pattern graduates from "watching" to "confirmed" at 3+ independent instances.)

1. **82% stayed = the breakout bar (confirmed Oct 15):** Oxygen Mask (83.4%) and Mind Reading (82.4%) both broke out; Zero Gravity (81.6%) sat just under across 4 check-ins and held without tripping the alert; Theft Parachute (58.1%, finalized — Oct 16 read removed the "still processing" asterisk, direction unchanged) flatlined in distribution and died — five consistent instances, two above and three below. The bar holds in both directions. **Oct 19 sixth instance:** D.B. Cooper finalized at 71.2% stayed — below the bar — and the push never came (15.7K views vs 36.1K–94.2K typical, 'Views are lower than usual'). **Oct 19 ~50h: still no late push (15.9K vs 39K–97K typical, ranked 9 of 10) — the bar's prediction held into day three.** **Oct 20 ~61h: still no push (16.5K vs 41K–97.4K typical), stayed% rock-stable at 71.3% — the bar's prediction now holds into the third day on six consistent reads.** **Oct 22 SEVENTH instance (early, retention still processing): Lucky Coin Dilemma at 59.8% stayed — the second independent video in Theft Parachute's 58.1% death band. - **Format notes:** FRAME ZERO VERIFIED by direct playback inspection (Oct 22 evening run): Roblox street scene (crosswalk, tree, storefronts with orange-and-white awnings); foreground avatar — black spiky hair, goggles on forehead, orange-black striped jacket — holds up a glowing gold coin with a $ symbol, floating dollar bill beside it; smiling brown-haired avatar in black hoodie behind; burned-in "Would" near bottom-center at 0:00 (+ @wasglyph watermark) unfolding to "Would you take unlimited luck if…" by ~4s; NO choice/dilemma panel or visible cost/twist in the first frame. Diagnosis: the frame sells the prize, not the choice — it reads as a promise, not a decision; the twist condition arrives ~4s+, after the swipe window. Notable counterpoint for H12 (provisional): D.B. Cooper's fully-empty opening (no premise visible at 0:00, Mission 003 verified) finalized at 71.2% stayed — HIGHER than this hook-starting, prize-visible opening — so the visible element may need to be the dilemma (choice + cost), not just the question's first word or the prize. Watching, not doctrine. Oct 23 evening: comment mining confirms the body does hook the mechanic for stayers (22/22 top-level comments are wish-scaling loopholes, zero off-premise) — but with 0.19% comment rate the mechanic engages too few viewers to matter; the loss is still at the swipe.

## Part 2 — Niche pop-offs (competitor blow-ups)

Every niche pop-off (~3x+ baseline engagement) the daily competitor tripwire catches, broken down the same way every time. The point: after enough entries, patterns emerge that no single alert can show.

## Entry format (every pop-off gets all fields)

- **Date:**
- **Creator:** (platform: IG / YT)
- **Post:**
- **Metrics:** likes, comments
- **Baseline multiple:** vs. creator's recent norms
- **Hook:** first 1–3 seconds / title — what stops the scroll
- **Mechanic:** the core engine of the piece
- **Escalation / Twist:** how it builds, where it lands
- **Why it worked:** the underlying psychology, in one or two lines
- **Steal-able for:** @wasglyph / @notglyph + which lane
- **Tags:**

## Part 2 — Emerging patterns

> **AUDIT NOTE (Oct 20, 2026, Mission 002):** Pattern labels below use the retired Oct 2026 standard ("confirmed" at 3+ instances). Under the current standard (methodology/research_rules.md §11–13), every pattern here is a **hypothesis** with a confidence level — see analysis/hypotheses.md for the scored register, counterexamples, and independent-video sample sizes. Original text preserved; nothing deleted.

(Update this section as entries accumulate. A pattern graduates from "watching" to "confirmed" after 3+ independent instances.)

> **Oct 20 ~5:40 PM run update:** `post_created_at` was Unavailable for every IG post pulled today (provider regression — even single-post reads returned `created: null`), so 48h recency is unverified for all new entries; multiples are Calculated vs creator recent-post medians and all entries carry date caveats. New Part 2 entries: 14 (blooporobloxs Lie Counter 4.7x; noobifieed fear→superpower 10.7x comments — account back after ~29 days quiet; ricarro_yt teacher-prank POV 12.8x; robloxdevverse Monster Closet 14.0x comments + TRAIN 7.1x comments; onvi.shorts Rich Kid karma 6.6x/52x comments + Knife-out-of-poop 3.5x; craftee_films If You Move You Turn Invisible 3.1x — grew into the line from 1.96x; emori_films THE SHADOW 7.3x + SOULMATE TRACKER 4.4x + THE WORD 4.0x; imcrellar He Should Have Listened 4.4x; dylanstoriesyt Wake Up 5.1x; hisbloxy Everything I Draw Comes To Life 3.0x borderline). Growth on prior pop-offs (no new entries): capybluh sock 15x→**51.4x** still climbing, Microwave 13.7K→14.2K likes; nepshublox 🗿 now 137x, Engine Snail 5.1K→7.1K likes, Ghost Fish 1.96K→4.0K; onvi.shorts Parrot 3.0x→14.3x, FBI Kid 11.3K→17.0K likes (~54x); roblxify feathers riddle 1,585→1,882 comments, bird puzzle 476→547, murder-alibi 566→687; dylanstoriesyt Barcode 11.5x→12.0x; hisbloxy Nobody Would Sit With Him 7.6K→46.0K likes (~145x). **Correction:** today's roblxify feathers/bird flags are the SAME Oct 15 posts still growing — NOT new 13th/14th instances; the hidden-detail comment-bait count stands at 12+ instances. Pattern evidence deltas: **velocity clustering** — craftee_films' baseline median is now 37,246 likes (the hot streak is compounding the account's whole floor, not just hits); dylanstoriesyt's cluster is now 4 independent videos (+Wake Up). **Single-rule worlds** — 8th craftee instance (Invisible) + TWO independent-creator instances (robloxdevverse Monster Closet + TRAIN) + noobifieed fear→superpower (10.7x comments) — the one-creator confound is weakening; still a hypothesis per §11–13, confidence rising toward moderate. **True-crime retelling** — onvi.shorts Rich Kid karma = 2nd instance same creator; Parrot still growing (3.0x→14.3x) — still one creator, stays watching. **Everyday-mechanics explainers** — capybluh sock (51x) + Microwave (11x) = 2nd same-creator instance — stays watching, needs an independent creator. **Tragic-loss** — imcrellar "He Should Have Listened" = 8th instance, still one creator (repost-aggregator caveat). **New watching item — boom-bust distribution:** emori_films logged 3 hits at 4.0–7.3x while its newest 3 reels sit at 0.008–0.03x of baseline — the same account's floor collapsed. Counterpoint to velocity clustering: distribution appears to follow the hit, not the account. First instance; watch for repeats.

> **Nov 8 ~5:40 PM run update:** `created_at` working — 48h recency verified for every post this run (window Nov 6 22:40 → Nov 8 22:40 UTC; collection ~22:40 UTC). Baseline window: last-20 reels per account, sorted by `created_at` descending. **MEDIAN REBOUND — craftee_films' last-20 medians ROSE this run (likes 40,161 → 48,329, +20%; comments 139.5 → 199, +43%), the mirror image of Nov 7's 51%/41% fall — so no crossing this run is median-assisted.** New Part 2 entries: 2. **craftee_films "I Was Too Tall To Hide 😳"** (250,870 likes, 5.19x / 822 comments, 4.13x — ROBUST both axes, first read at trip level). **fearless.gman "Who is The Woman In My Photos!😨"** (1,760 likes, 6.58x — ROBUST likes-axis, growth into the bar from Nov 7's 481-like (2.02x) near-trip read, +266% in ~24h, same media ID; 1. **Escalation chains (CONFIRMED):** mundane trigger → absurd, ever-bigger consequences. nepshublox fan-police (182x), fearless.gman impostor surge (152x), nepshublox boxing (81x), nepshublox parking-spot feud (26x, Oct 12 — 5th big hit in ~2 weeks for this account), craftee_films sleepwalking night-shift (16.7x, Oct 13 — Army cot → hurricane → national debt paid → President reviews footage), nepshublox grandma (27.3x, Oct 13 — bat → revolver → bazooka → orchestrated switch), craftee_films chess-accident (32.8x, Oct 16 — luck-escalation underdog comedy; tripled from 117K to 357K likes in 24h). 8+ instances across 2 creators. Comedy lane gold for @notglyph.
2. **Inverted goals (CONFIRMED):** win by losing / last place wins. craftee_films race (20.9x), nepshublox boxing (81x). Proven across 2+ creators — strong @notglyph mechanic.
3. **Velocity clustering (CONFIRMED):** once a creator pops off twice, the next 2–3 posts ride the wave — the algorithm keeps feeding the hot account. nepshublox: boxing (81x) → fan-police (182x) → parking spot (26x) → grandma (27.3x) + bank sting (19.5x) → **4 fresh hits Oct 15–16 (DON'T CHECK THE SOUND 8.9x, W Titan Planet X 5.5x, Engine Snail 36.1x→46.8x, Limited Weapons ranking 7.6x) → Oct 17–18: 🗿 meme post (119.9x likes / 81.3x comments, account's 2nd-biggest multiple ever caught) + Divine Voidseer Pumpkin Egg (13.2x)** — 11+ hits in ~2 weeks, and the hot streak now extends to pure coverage posts AND opaque meme captions, not just comedy; craftee_films: race (20.9x) → aura (7.1x) → pilot (4.6x) + blink (11.8x) → night-shift (16.7x), crush (8.2x), jealous (7.4x), hitman (13.1x), LOVE LETTERS (6.8x), doctor's coat (4.5x), IKEA (4.0x comments) — 11 pop-offs in 4 days, and STILL running: BEST FRIENDS (7.6x, Oct 15) → Couple's Discount (3.9x comments, Oct 15) → chess champion (5.4x→**32.8x, Oct 16 — TRIPLED in 24h**) → She Never Says No (9.2x, Oct 16) → One Tower (3.2x→**15.4x, Oct 17–18 — grew 4.9x in 24h**) → **Oct 18: 1M Steps (9.8x), Teleport Every Hour (8.6x), Certain Material (7.7x), MAFIA GIRLFRIEND (5.3x comments), ROBOT GIRLFRIEND (3.0x)** → **Oct 19: the three Oct-18 world-rule posts grew AGAIN in 24h (Steps 106.8K→304.7K, +2.85x; Material +1.78x; Teleport +1.6x) and a comment-driven SCARY GIRL tripped at 3.2x** — **~18+ pop-offs in 8 days**, with the hot-streak compounding the back catalog, not just new posts; fearless.gman impostor surge (4+ hits since Oct 3). **dylanstoriesyt holds the club (Oct 10–17): Gold Coins (3.25x) → Barcode (11.5x) → Infinite Buffet (3.0x→3.8x, still climbing) — 3 pop-offs, all still rising.** **nepshublox's streak now covers whole lanes, not hits: two separate Halloween-fishing coverage posts (Ghost Fish 9.8x, Halloween Fishing 7.9x) tripped on the same night, Oct 18–19.** **Nov 6: streak stays live against a SHARPLY RISING median (likes 53,977 → 81,295.5, comments 145.5 → 238 in 24h) — BRO CODE tripped comments 4.18x and 8 prior pop-offs grew, but Fish fell below the bar (2.52x) on the median rise alone; the streak lifts the floor so fast that yesterday's trips become today's near-trips.** Actionable: when a competitor is hot, their NEXT posts get distribution — watch their next 48h for steal-able formats before they peak.

(hisbloxy "I Destroyed The Class Clown 🤡" Oct 11, 12.9x, is a possible 3rd instance — story beats unverified, lane fit unclear; stays watching until verified.)

(11.8x, Oct 13) — a town where blinking is forbidden; "The IKEA" (4.0x comments, Oct 14) — infinite-IKEA survival with the "store is now closed" announcement, riding borrowed SCP-3008 lore; "Everyone Lives In One Tower" (**167.5K likes, ~15.4x, Oct 17–18 — grew 4.9x in 24h**) — a dystopian world-rule stated as fact; **Oct 18 triples the count in 24h: "In This Town, You Get 1,000,000 Steps" (9.8x), "Every Person Is Made Of A Certain Material" (7.7x), "You Teleport Every Hour" (8.6x).** **Oct 19: the formula cluster keeps widening — "If You Move You Turn invisible" (Oct 19, 1.96x likes, below the pop-off line) is the 7th single-rule premise in ~36h; even the ones that don't trip are getting distribution.** 6+ instances, same creator — still needs an independent creator to graduate. This account's current formula: second-person quantified world-rules ("You Teleport Every Hour" — the viewer IS the subject). @notglyph lane — quantified rules beat abstract ones. **Nov 6 counterexample: craftee's "In This World The School Lunch Lady Can Make Anything" (Nov 5) sits at 1.21x likes / 0.72x comments — a single-rule premise that did NOT trip, inside the hot account.**
6. **Visible-stat gags (watching):** a quantified stat shown on screen drives the comedy. craftee_films aura-overflow skit (7.1x, Oct 12) — aura score cascades negative, then overflows. First instance; watch for repeats.
7. **True-crime retelling (watching):** a real crime story retold in Roblox animation. onvi.shorts "FBI's Most Wanted KID" (7.3x, Oct 12) — teen plane-thief saga. Fits onvi's crime lane; watch for repeats.

(15x, Oct 13) — lost sock → hamster sleeping inside it → cheese bribe. First instance; watch for repeats. Weak fit for @wasglyph, warm micro-story lane for @notglyph.
9. **Disguise-reveal comedy (watching):** dramatic-irony identity concealment with a physical reveal. craftee_films "My Crush Dressed As A Boy" (8.2x likes, 11.8x comments, Oct 13) — hug-and-back-slap until a pillow knocks the wig off. First instance; watch for repeats.

11. **Tragic-loss payoff (watching):** devastating gaming loss / grief IS the whole story. imcrellar: Sep 23 tragic-loss reel (221K, since grown to 297K), Sep 21 "He Tamed The Hacker" (18.9x), Sep 29 "Thrown Across The Room" (11.7x), Oct 1 "Anything For The Win" (8.0x), Sep 25 "He Went Down With His Ship" (7.0x), Aug 27 "FISHING GAME BTW" (21.9x). 7 instances, ONE creator — needs an independent creator to graduate. Grief is the strongest retention emotion when earned fast; high-risk lane, use sparingly.
12. **Identity-impostor dread (watching):** a familiar person appears where they can't be — the "then who are you?" payoff. fearless.gman: "My Dog is SMILING!" (Oct 11, 152x), "Was She A STALKER Or What?" (Oct 10, 35.6x), "She Knocked My Window At 2AM" (Oct 11, 5.3x). 3 instances, one creator — needs an independent creator to graduate. Identity violation is a primal fear; the "next room / 2AM window" framing makes it instantly visualizable. @notglyph Test 3 built on this; warm for the horror lane. **Nov 8: 4th instance — "Who is The Woman In My Photos!" (Nov 7, 6.6x likes, growth into the bar from a 481-like near-trip read; title-only, phantom-person-in-photos variant). Still one creator, stays watching.**
13. **Novelty-drop speed coverage (watching):** being first to cover a new game drop pops regardless of format. nepshublox: mounts-news (Oct 11), "I Equipped 4 Mutations on a Huge Celestial Sunlion" (6.1x, Oct 14), "W Titan Planet X Zone Break" (5.5x, Oct 15), "Engine Snail" mount + Divine World Burner heist (36.1x→46.8x, Oct 16, still rising), **"Divine Voidseer Pumpkin Egg" (13.2x likes / 9x comments, Oct 17 — Halloween drop coverage)**. **Oct 18–19 adds "Eternal Ghost Fish" (9.8x) and "Halloween Fishing is Fun" (7.9x) — 7 instances, ONE creator — needs an independent creator to graduate.** The audience treats the account as the niche news desk; speed beats production value.
14. **Relationship-premise storytimes (watching):** the title is the premise — "X GIRLFRIEND" / dating-someone frames. craftee_films: "She Never Says No" (9.2x likes, Oct 16), "MAFIA GIRLFRIEND" (5.3x comments, Oct 17 — a comment-driven pop-off), "ROBOT GIRLFRIEND" (3.0x likes, Oct 17). 3 instances, same creator — needs an independent creator to graduate. The premise does the work; the audience debates the fantasy in comments. @notglyph teen-romance lane.
15. **Everyday-mechanics explainers (watching):** a mundane object or process explained in animation, curiosity about something everyone uses but never examines. capybluh "How a Microwave Works" (5.8x, Oct 18 — the account's first confirmed explainer-format hit, after the 30x sock mystery; still climbing at the Oct 19 run). First instance; watch for repeats. Possible @notglyph novelty format; weak @wasglyph fit (no dilemma).
16. **Comments-outrun-likes trips (watching):** a post whose comments multiple clears 3x while its likes multiple stays merely notable (1–2.5x) — guess-who / stakes-question / unexplained-action / social-code titles drive comments past like appeal. craftee_films: Fox Returns (13.75x comments vs 2.04x likes), ONE OF US (3.68x vs 2.44x), NEVER LET GO (3.45x vs 1.61x), Fish (4.46x vs 1.55x), GOLDEN RAIN (3.46x vs 1.97x), THE ANGEL (4.27x vs 2.30x), THE BABYSIT (3.21x vs 1.17x), BRO CODE (4.18x vs 1.90x) — 8 instances, one creator. **Nov 6: first SECOND-creator instance — capybluh "Do not waste food" (3.22x comments vs 2.18x likes, borderline, growth into the bar).** Still a hypothesis (low confidence, rising toward moderate) — needs more independent creators before it graduates. Counterexamples: craftee's own likes-axis trips (KARMA 5.78x likes / 6.69x comments — both axes trip) show the shape is not universal. @notglyph lane — titles that make viewers post an answer, not just a reaction.
